10:class LoginScreen extends StatefulWidget {
11:  const LoginScreen({super.key});
12:
13:  @override
14:  State<LoginScreen> createState() => _LoginScreenState();
15:}
16:
17:class _LoginScreenState extends State<LoginScreen> {
18:  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
19:  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
20:  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();
21:  bool _isLoading = false;
22:  String? _errorMessage;
23:  bool _obscurePassword = true;
24:
25:  @override
26:  void dispose() {
27:    _emailController.dispose();
28:    _passwordController.dispose();
29:    super.dispose();
30:  }
31:
32:  Future<void> _handleLogin() async {
33:    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
34:
35:    if (!mounted) return;
36:    setState(() {
37:      _isLoading = true;
38:      _errorMessage = null;
39:    });
40:
41:    try {
42:      // Remove all spaces from email/username
43:      final loginInput = _emailController.text.replaceAll(' ', '').trim();
44:      
45:      final result = await locator.authRepository.login(
46:        loginInput,
47:        _passwordController.text,
48:      );
49:
50:      if (!mounted) return;
51:
52:      if (result.isSuccess) {
53:        // Navigate based on user role
54:        final user = locator.authRepository.currentUser;
55:        Widget nextScreen;
56:        
57:        if (user?.role == 'CAISSIER') {
58:          nextScreen = CaissierScreen();
59:        } else if (user?.role == 'GESTIONNAIRE') {
60:          nextScreen = GestionnaireScreen();
61:        } else if (user?.role == 'ADMINISTRATEUR' || user?.role == 'Administrateur') {
62:          nextScreen = AdminScreen();
63:        } else {
64:          // Default to home screen for unknown roles
65:          nextScreen = const HomeScreen();
66:        }
67:        
68:        if (mounted) {
69:          Navigator.pushReplacement(
70:            context,
71:            MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => nextScreen),
72:          );
73:        }
74:      } else {
75:        if (mounted) {
76:          setState(() {
77:            _errorMessage = result.errorMessage;
78:          });
79:        }
80:      }
81:    } catch (e) {
82:      if (mounted) {
83:        setState(() {
84:          _errorMessage = 'Erreur de connexion: $e';
85:        });
86:      }
87:    } finally {
88:      if (mounted) {
89:        setState(() {
90:          _isLoading = false;
91:        });
92:      }
93:    }
94:  }

419:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
420:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
421:
422:  // Extract initials from user's full name
423:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
424:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
425:    
426:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
427:    if (names.length >= 2) {
428:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
429:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
430:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
431:      // Take first two letters of the single name
432:      final name = names[0];
433:      if (name.length >= 2) {
434:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
435:      } else {
436:        return name.toUpperCase();
437:      }
438:    }
439:    return '??';
440:  }

704:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
705:
706:  // Extract initials from user's full name
707:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
708:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
709:    
710:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
711:    if (names.length >= 2) {
712:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
713:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
714:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
715:      // Take first two letters of the single name
716:      final name = names[0];
717:      if (name.length >= 2) {
718:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
719:      } else {
720:        return name.toUpperCase();
721:      }
722:    }
723:    return '??';
724:  }

1564:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
1565:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
1566:
1567:  // Extract initials from user's full name
1568:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
1569:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
1570:    
1571:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
1572:    if (names.length >= 2) {
1573:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
1574:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
1575:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
1576:      // Take first two letters of the single name
1577:      final name = names[0];
1578:      if (name.length >= 2) {
1579:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
1580:      } else {
1581:        return name.toUpperCase();
1582:      }
1583:    }
1584:    return '??';
1585:  }

2156:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
2157:    
2158:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
2159:    if (names.length >= 2) {
2160:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
2161:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';

2163:      // Take first two letters of the single name
2164:      final name = names[0];

2166:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
2167:      } else {
2168:        return name.toUpperCase();
2169:      }
2170:    }

2729:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
2730:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
2731:
2732:  // Extract initials from user's full name
2733:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
2734:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
2735:    
2736:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
2737:    if (names.length >= 2) {
2738:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
2739:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
2740:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
2741:      // Take first two letters of the single name
2742:      final name = names[0];
2743:      if (name.length >= 2) {
2744:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
2745:      } else {
2746:        return name.toUpperCase();
2747:      }
2748:    }
2749:    return '??';
2750:  }

4156:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
4157:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
4158:
4159:  // Extract initials from user's full name
4160:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
4161:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
4162:    
4163:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
4164:    if (names.length >= 2) {
4165:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
4166:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
4167:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
4168:      // Take first two letters of the single name
4169:      final name = names[0];
4170:      if (name.length >= 2) {
4171:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
4172:      } else {
4173:        return name.toUpperCase();
4174:      }
4175:    }
4176:    return '??';
4177:  }

4743:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
4744:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
4745:
4746:  // Extract initials from user's full name
4747:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
4748:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
4749:    
4750:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
4751:    if (names.length >= 2) {
4752:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
4753:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
4754:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
4755:      // Take first two letters of the single name
4756:      final name = names[0];
4757:      if (name.length >= 2) {
4758:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
4759:      } else {
4760:        return name.toUpperCase();
4761:      }
4762:    }
4763:    return '??';
4764:  }

5321:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
5322:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
5323:
5324:  // Extract initials from user's full name
5325:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
5326:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
5327:    
5328:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
5329:    if (names.length >= 2) {
5330:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
5331:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
5332:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
5333:      // Take first two letters of the single name
5334:      final name = names[0];
5335:      if (name.length >= 2) {
5336:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
5337:      } else {
5338:        return name.toUpperCase();
5339:      }
5340:    }
5341:    return '??';
5342:  }

6748:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
6749:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
6750:
6751:  // Extract initials from user's full name
6752:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
6753:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
6754:    
6755:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
6756:    if (names.length >= 2) {
6757:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
6758:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
6759:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
6760:      // Take first two letters of the single name
6761:      final name = names[0];
6762:      if (name.length >= 2) {
6763:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
6764:      } else {
6765:        return name.toUpperCase();
6766:      }
6767:    }
6768:    return '??';
6769:  }

7335:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
7336:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
7337:
7338:  // Extract initials from user's full name
7339:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
7340:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
7341:    
7342:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
7343:    if (names.length >= 2) {
7344:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
7345:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
7346:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
7347:      // Take first two letters of the single name
7348:      final name = names[0];
7349:      if (name.length >= 2) {
7350:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
7351:      } else {
7352:        return name.toUpperCase();
7353:      }
7354:    }
7355:    return '??';
7356:  }

7913:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
7914:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
7915:
7916:  // Extract initials from user's full name
7917:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
7918:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
7919:    
7920:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
7921:    if (names.length >= 2) {
7922:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
7923:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
7924:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
7925:      // Take first two letters of the single name
7926:      final name = names[0];
7927:      if (name.length >= 2) {
7928:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
7929:      } else {
7930:        return name.toUpperCase();
7931:      }
7932:    }
7933:    return '??';
7934:  }

8479:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
8480:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
8481:
8482:  // Extract initials from user's full name
8483:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
8484:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
8485:    
8486:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
8487:    if (names.length >= 2) {
8488:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
8489:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
8490:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
8491:      // Take first two letters of the single name
8492:      final name = names[0];
8493:      if (name.length >= 2) {
8494:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
8495:      } else {
8496:        return name.toUpperCase();
8497:      }
8498:    }
8499:    return '??';
8500:  }

9060:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
9061:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
9062:    
9063:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
9064:    if (names.length >= 2) {
9065:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
9066:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
9067:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
9068:      // Take first two letters of the single name
9069:      final name = names[0];
9070:      if (name.length >= 2) {
9071:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
9072:      } else {
9073:        return name.toUpperCase();
9074:      }
9075:    }
9076:    return '??';
9077:  }

9927:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
9928:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
9929:
9930:  // Extract initials from user's full name
9931:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
9932:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
9933:    
9934:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
9935:    if (names.length >= 2) {
9936:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
9937:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
9938:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
9939:      // Take first two letters of the single name
9940:      final name = names[0];
9941:      if (name.length >= 2) {
9942:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
9943:      } else {
9944:        return name.toUpperCase();
9945:      }
9946:    }
9947:    return '??';
9948:  }

10339:                                    });
10340:                                  },

10343:                                    color: const Color(0xFF2C3E50),
10344:                                  ),
10345:                                ),
10346:                                filled: true,
10347:                                fillColor: Colors.grey[50],
10348:                              ),
10349:                              obscureText: _obscurePassword,
10350:                              textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
10351:                              onFieldSubmitted: (_) => _handleLogin(),
10352:                              validator: (value) {
10353:                                if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
10354:                                  return 'Veuillez entrer votre mot de passe';
10355:                                }

10504:
10505:class HomeScreenWidget extends StatelessWidget {
10506:  const HomeScreenWidget({super.key});
10507:
10508:  // Extract initials from user's full name
10509:  String _getUserInitials(String fullName) {
10510:    if (fullName.isEmpty) return '??';
10511:    
10512:    final names = fullName.trim().split(' ');
10513:    if (names.length >= 2) {
10514:      // Take first letter of first name and first letter of last name
10515:      return '${names[0][0].toUpperCase()}${names[names.length - 1][0].toUpperCase()}';
10516:    } else if (names.isNotEmpty) {
10517:      // Take first two letters of the single name
10518:      final name = names[0];
10519:      if (name.length >= 2) {
10520:        return '${name[0].toUpperCase()}${name[1].toUpperCase()}';
10521:      } else {
10522:        return name.toUpperCase();
10523:      }
10524:    }
10525:    return '??';
10526:  }
10527:
10528:  @override
10529:  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
10530:    return Scaffold(
10531:      appBar: AppBar(
10532:        title: const Text('Mombongo - Accueil'),
10533:        backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.inversePrimary,
10534:        actions: [
10535:          IconButton(

10537:              radius: 16,
10538:              backgroundColor: Colors.white,
10539:              child: Text(
10540:                _getUserInitials(locator.authRepository.currentUser?.nomComplet ?? ''),
10541:                style: TextStyle(
10542:                  color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
10543:                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
10544:                  fontSize: 14,
10545:                ),
10546:              ),
10547:            ),

10549:              // Navigate to profile screen

10553:              );
10554:            },
10555:          ),

10558:            onPressed: () async {
10559:              await locator.authRepository.logout();
10560:              if (context.mounted) {
10561:                Navigator.pushReplacement(
10562:                  context,
10563:                  MaterialPageRoute(builder: (context) => const LoginScreen()),
10564:                );
10565:              }
10566:            },
10567:          ),
